Turkey to the chairman of the Grand National Assembly With respect to the opening of the Parliament's research commission in accordance with Articles 104, 105, our 98th and Interior Charter 104, in order to determine and investigate the ways of the pensioners' financial and spiritual problems. [[Artvin]] [[Denizli]] [[Slap]] [[İstanbul]] [[Scholarship]] [[Scholarship]] [[Manisa]] [[40s.]] [[Sivas]] [[İstanbul]] 11) Tur Star Reaper [[Manisa]] 12) Mustafa Sezgin Tanrıkulu [[İstanbul]] [[Ankara]] [[Tekirdağ]] [[Aydın]] [[Balıkesir]] [[Ankara]] [[Eskişehir]] [[Malatya]] [[İstanbul]] [[40s.]] [[Zonguldak]] [[Burdur]] The problems of pensioners who have been retired in our country, who have struggled to live on the edge of poverty and hunger, who have struggled to live on the welfare level for the rest of their lives have been ignored for years, and this problem has not been resolved. In our country, where there are nearly 11 million pensioners, AKP power has made many promises in each election, but pensioners' problems have not been resolved. There are major gaps between a retired person and a working person. This gap between your salaries must be repaired immediately. In the last spring of his life, people need to live comfortably, have the economy to provide for health and social needs. Although the social security system is collected under one roof, the inequality between incomes and monthlys continues. The fact that there are inequalities in terms of income and monthly rates is increasing the differences that exist in each increase. For example, the lack of disparity in base monthly points out to be an important problem. Primal earnings and premium paydays should be the basic criteria for calculating pensions, but the cost-effective balance of benefits and different monthly payments are being paid because pensions are calculated according to the laws of the period, which have often been adopted as a result of changing legislation. Even though they are in the same status as norms and standard unity in the method of calculating pensions, the inequality of pensions is growing. In particular, there are monthly reductions in pensioners, including 4/A and 4/B, following 2008. Therefore, the pension parameters of Social Insurance Act 5510 and the General Health Insurance Act must be reevaluated and paid the same month, regardless of the number of days to pay the premiums and the years when they retire. I mean, a man should get the same salary when he retires, no matter how much he gets paid before he retires. Besides, pensioners shouldn't be wasted in public hospitals. He must take advantage of all private hospitals, public hospitals, the same way they can. A pensioner should not be taxed on the use of services such as transportation, fuel. In short, in the sense of social government, the state shouldn't punish pensioners, it should be rewarded for serving them all these years. MADDE Article 119 of Law 12-2709 has been replaced in the following manner and the border headers have been removed from the text. ELLI. State of emergency management MADDE 119- The President may declare an extraordinary state of state in case of war, war, war, mobilisation, riots, a strong and action against the country or the Republic, the spread of acts of violence that endangers the country's undistribution from within or outside, constitutional order or widespread acts of violence aimed at eliminating fundamental rights and freedoms, in the event of a severe disaster or severe economic crisis. It is published in the official newspaper on the day of the State of Emergency decision and is submitted to the approval of the Grand National Assembly of Turkey on the same day. If Turkey is on holiday, the Grand National Assembly of Turkey is called to a meeting immediately; Parliament can shorten, extend, or remove the state of emergency if necessary. At the request of the president, the Turkish Grand National Assembly can extend the deadline for four months at a time. In cases of war, that is not a four-month period. In extraordinary cases, the money for citizens is regulated by law in accordance with the principles of goods and labour obligations and the principles of Article 15 on which basic rights and freedoms will be limited or temporarily halted, which provisions will be carried out and how procedures will be carried out. In extraordinary cases, the President may issue a presidential decree on matters which are essential to the state of emergency, without subject to restrictions stated in the second sentence of Article 104's 17th joke. These decrees under the law are published in the official newspaper and submitted to Parliament's approval on the same day. The presidential decrees issued during the state of emergency are held within three months in Turkey's Grand National Assembly, excluding the failure of the Grand National Assembly of Turkey for war and murky reasons. Otherwise, the presidential decree, which has been issued in extraordinary situations, will automatically be lifted. The first statement on the 12th article is from Ankara MP Efendi Cetin (Kemet Çetin) on behalf of the National Movement Party Group. Here you go, Mr. Cetin.

The amendment to the 12th article of the proposal has been met under one clause in the extraordinary state and martial law regulations of our current Constitution, which are included in Article 119, 120, 121, and 122. The provision of martial law has been completely removed from our Constitution. It is up to the House's discretion that both the state of emergency and the state of emergency continue. It has also required that the state of emergency be published on the same day and again given to Parliament's approval on the same day. The presidential decrees issued during the state of emergency are held within three months and will be held in parliament, except for the failure of the Parliament to meet for war and reason. In the current system, our parliament has two main tasks; the first is the legislature, and the second is the administrative duty. tbmm-2017-01-14sit06spe0517par0038	Shame on you, Mr. Planter! MADDE 15-2709 has been changed in the following way, along with the title of Article 161 of the Law. ▪ A. Budget and final account MADDE 161- The expenditures of public legal persons outside public administrations and public economic initiatives are carried out by annual budgets. Special deadlines and procedures for the start of the fiscal year and the draft, implementation and control of the central management budget and investments or services that will last more than a year are organised by law. The budget law shall have no provisions other than budgetary provisions. The President submits the budget law proposal to the Grand National Assembly of Turkey at least seventy-five days before fiscal Christmas. The budget offer is discussed at the Budget Commission. The text the commission will accept within fifty-five days will be discussed in the General Assembly, and it will be decided by fiscal New Year's Eve. If the budget law is not implemented during the duration, the temporary budget law will be passed. If the temporary budget law is not adopted, the budget of the previous year will be applied according to the rate of revaluement until the new budget law is adopted. Members of the Grand National Assembly of Turkey explain their views on public administration budgets at each time the budget is discussed, they cannot offer extenuating or reducing revenues. The General Assembly has proposed amendments to public administration budgets, which are also read and voted on without discussion. The funding given by the central management budget shows the limit of the amount available. The budget law cannot be judged as to how much can be spent on the presidential decree. It is imperative that financial resources be provided to meet the envisioned costs, as well as to offer changes in the annual budget, which bring financial burden to the current budgets of years and years to follow. The Central Government draft law proposal is submitted by the President to the Grand National Assembly of Turkey six months later, starting at the end of its fiscal year. The Court will submit its statement of general fitness to parliament within 75 days, starting with the proposal of a definite bill. The proposal for the final account law and the general declaration of fitness were submitted to the Turkish Grand National Assembly, which does not prevent an unsolved audit and account justice of the following year, and does not mean that they have been reached. The proposal for the final accounting law is discussed and settled along with the new year's budget law proposal. tbmm-2017-01-14sit06spe0540par0171	Mr. President, thank you. To answer the questions of our representatives in general, first of all, there is no interference in the constitutional proposal, and the neutrality of the judiciary is clearly under constitutional control. According to Article 104 of our current Constitutional Court, it is the duty of the President to select a section of the Constitutional Court, a quarter of the People's Court, the Supreme Prosecutor's Attorney's Office and the Supreme Court of the Supreme Court, members of the Military Supreme Court and members of the Supreme Court of Judges and Prosecutors. The president will not be able to elect a member of these courts, as the Military Supreme Court and the Military Supreme Administration Court will be lifted by the proposal. Other than that, he does not have a new appointment or position given to the president in the field of justice. After the proposal's legislation, the president will continue to elect a section of the Constitutional Court members, one-quarter of the People's Court members, the Supreme Court's Attorney General and four members of the Council of Judges and Prosecutors, as predicted today. In fact, according to the current Constitutional Court, the President has the opportunity to appoint a member of the Supreme Court of the Military Court and the Military Supreme Administration Court, while the opportunity is being eliminated due to the removal of the military judiciary. As it turns out, there is no increase in the President's duties and powers regarding the judiciary. Therefore, allegations that the proposal destroyed judicial independence are completely baseless. On the other hand, we are saying from the very beginning that the powers of the legislature have been strengthened within the Constitutional Proposal. With the proposal brought in, the Council of Ministers will be terminated and the administration will not come out of the law. As a natural result, the vote of confidence, the geneor and the bill are removed from the Constitution. The president will not be able to make a law proposal except in budget and final account proposals; only lawmakers will be able to offer law with the regulation of Articles 87 and 88 of the Constitution. Depending on the government system brought in, the vote of geneor and trust has been removed from the Constitution. In this way, the public will give the gene, and the vote of confidence will be taken from the public. The parliamentary investigation, parliamentary investigation, general discussion and written question life continues from the existing ways of learning and auditing the parliament. More importantly, the Parliament's laws, amendments and lifts in Article 87 of the Constitution continue in the same way. As the Council of Ministers has been suspended due to the removal of the Council of Ministers, the President's decision of the presidency is envisioned within the limits envisioned in the Constitution. The President's decision cannot be made to regulate the issues regarding basic rights and freedoms, and the presidential decree cannot be passed on the issues organised by the law. In 82 separate articles of our current Constitution, the obligation to regulate certain issues has been enforced by law. In addition to basic rights and freedoms, the presidential decree is not possible. Thus, it is clear that the offer is not only protecting the powers of the legislature today, but it also clearly regulates the sharing of authority between the legislative and executive organs. Thank you.
